完整修改前後對照

短網址、直接下載 QR code、VPIRS-E 待確認計分、學習滿意度與混合題型。題目來源不含任何歷史填答；名冊與個人成績不列於此頁。

components/auth-portal.tsx

修改前（完整檔案）

import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { BackendLogin } from '@/components/backend-login';
import { StudentLogin, StudentActivation, ResetStudentPassword, type Onboarding } from '@/components/student-access';
import { DEMO_MODE, demoUser } from '@/lib/demo';
import { rpc, supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { LoginPanel, PortalShell } from '@/components/portal-shell';
import { REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION } from '@/lib/schema';
import '@/app/surveys.css';

const BackendWorkspace = lazy(()=>import('@/components/backend-workspace').then(module=>({default:module.BackendWorkspace})));
const BackendApplication = lazy(()=>import('@/components/backend-accounts').then(module=>({default:module.BackendApplication})));
const StudentDashboard = lazy(()=>import('@/app/student/student-dashboard'));
const SurveyLinkPage = lazy(()=>import('@/components/student-surveys').then(module=>({default:module.SurveyLinkPage})));

export function AuthPortal({ teacher = false }: { teacher?: boolean }) {
  const surveyAssignmentId = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('survey');
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ role: 'teacher' | 'student'; username: string; email: string } | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Onboarding | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(''), [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [recovery, setRecovery] = useState(new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('reset') === '1');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (DEMO_MODE) { setUser(demoUser()); return; }
    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, current) => {
      if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') setRecovery(true);
      setSession(current);
    });
    return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);
  const email = session?.user.email, uid = session?.user.id;
  const sessionReady = session !== undefined;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (DEMO_MODE || !sessionReady) return;
    if (!email) { setUser(null); setStatus(null); setError(''); return; }
    let active = true;
    setUser(undefined); setError('');
    (async () => {
      try {
        const schema = await rpc<number>('nptc_schema_version');
        if (schema < REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION) throw new Error('資料庫版本尚未更新，請聯絡系統管理者完成升級。');
        const isTeacher = await rpc<boolean>('nptc_is_teacher');
        if (!isTeacher && !recovery) await rpc('nptc_sync_student_email');
        const onboarding = !teacher && !isTeacher && !recovery ? await rpc<Onboarding>('nptc_student_onboarding_status') : null;
        if (active) { setStatus(onboarding); setUser({ role: isTeacher ? 'teacher' : 'student', username: email, email }); }
      } catch (cause) { if (active) setError((cause as Error).message); }
    })();
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [email, uid, sessionReady, teacher, version, recovery]);
  const reload = () => setVersion(v => v + 1);
  return <PortalShell email={user?.username ?? email} teacher={teacher}>
    {DEMO_MODE && <p className="survey-local-notice" role="status">本機展示模式：所有填答僅存於此瀏覽器，請使用示範資料。</p>}
    <Suspense fallback={<p role="status" className="px-6 py-12 text-center">正在載入頁面…</p>}>
    {!teacher && surveyAssignmentId && !user && <p className="survey-login-notice">請先使用學員 Email 與密碼登入，登入後會接續開啟這份問卷。</p>}
    {error ? <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl space-y-4 px-6 py-12" role="alert"><p>{error}</p><button className="underline" onClick={reload}>重新檢查</button></div>
    : user === undefined ? <p className="px-6 py-12 text-center">正在確認登入狀態…</p>
    : !user ? <>{recovery && <p className="p-4 text-center" role="alert">請開啟最新的重設密碼信件；連結失效時可重新申請。</p>}{!DEMO_MODE ? (teacher ? <BackendLogin /> : <StudentLogin />) : <LoginPanel teacher={teacher} />}</>
    : recovery ? <ResetStudentPassword teacher={teacher} onComplete={() => { setRecovery(false); reload(); }} />
    : teacher && user.role !== 'teacher' ? <BackendApplication email={user.email} />
    : teacher ? <BackendWorkspace key={user.email} />
    : !DEMO_MODE && user.role !== 'teacher' && status?.stage !== 'active' ? <StudentActivation key={user.email} email={user.email} status={status ?? { stage: 'unclaimed' }} onComplete={reload} />
    : surveyAssignmentId ? <SurveyLinkPage key={`${user.email}-${surveyAssignmentId}`} assignmentId={surveyAssignmentId}/>
    : <div className="workspace"><StudentDashboard /></div>}
    </Suspense>
  </PortalShell>;
}

修改後（完整檔案）

import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { BackendLogin } from '@/components/backend-login';
import { StudentLogin, StudentActivation, ResetStudentPassword, type Onboarding } from '@/components/student-access';
import { DEMO_MODE, demoUser } from '@/lib/demo';
import { rpc, supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { LoginPanel, PortalShell } from '@/components/portal-shell';
import { REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION } from '@/lib/schema';
import '@/app/surveys.css';

const BackendWorkspace = lazy(()=>import('@/components/backend-workspace').then(module=>({default:module.BackendWorkspace})));
const BackendApplication = lazy(()=>import('@/components/backend-accounts').then(module=>({default:module.BackendApplication})));
const StudentDashboard = lazy(()=>import('@/app/student/student-dashboard'));
const SurveyLinkPage = lazy(()=>import('@/components/student-surveys').then(module=>({default:module.SurveyLinkPage})));

export function AuthPortal({ teacher = false, shortAssignmentId }: { teacher?: boolean; shortAssignmentId?: string }) {
  const surveyAssignmentId = shortAssignmentId ?? new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('survey');
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ role: 'teacher' | 'student'; username: string; email: string } | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Onboarding | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(''), [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [recovery, setRecovery] = useState(new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('reset') === '1');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (DEMO_MODE) { setUser(demoUser()); return; }
    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, current) => {
      if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') setRecovery(true);
      setSession(current);
    });
    return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);
  const email = session?.user.email, uid = session?.user.id;
  const sessionReady = session !== undefined;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (DEMO_MODE || !sessionReady) return;
    if (!email) { setUser(null); setStatus(null); setError(''); return; }
    let active = true;
    setUser(undefined); setError('');
    (async () => {
      try {
        const schema = await rpc<number>('nptc_schema_version');
        if (schema < REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION) throw new Error('資料庫版本尚未更新，請聯絡系統管理者完成升級。');
        const isTeacher = await rpc<boolean>('nptc_is_teacher');
        if (!isTeacher && !recovery) await rpc('nptc_sync_student_email');
        const onboarding = !teacher && !isTeacher && !recovery ? await rpc<Onboarding>('nptc_student_onboarding_status') : null;
        if (active) { setStatus(onboarding); setUser({ role: isTeacher ? 'teacher' : 'student', username: email, email }); }
      } catch (cause) { if (active) setError((cause as Error).message); }
    })();
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [email, uid, sessionReady, teacher, version, recovery]);
  const reload = () => setVersion(v => v + 1);
  return <PortalShell email={user?.username ?? email} teacher={teacher}>
    {DEMO_MODE && <p className="survey-local-notice" role="status">本機展示模式：所有填答僅存於此瀏覽器，請使用示範資料。</p>}
    <Suspense fallback={<p role="status" className="px-6 py-12 text-center">正在載入頁面…</p>}>
    {!teacher && surveyAssignmentId && !user && <p className="survey-login-notice">請先使用學員 Email 與密碼登入，登入後會接續開啟這份問卷。</p>}
    {error ? <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl space-y-4 px-6 py-12" role="alert"><p>{error}</p><button className="underline" onClick={reload}>重新檢查</button></div>
    : user === undefined ? <p className="px-6 py-12 text-center">正在確認登入狀態…</p>
    : !user ? <>{recovery && <p className="p-4 text-center" role="alert">請開啟最新的重設密碼信件；連結失效時可重新申請。</p>}{!DEMO_MODE ? (teacher ? <BackendLogin /> : <StudentLogin />) : <LoginPanel teacher={teacher} />}</>
    : recovery ? <ResetStudentPassword teacher={teacher} onComplete={() => { setRecovery(false); reload(); }} />
    : teacher && user.role !== 'teacher' ? <BackendApplication email={user.email} />
    : teacher ? <BackendWorkspace key={user.email} />
    : !DEMO_MODE && user.role !== 'teacher' && status?.stage !== 'active' ? <StudentActivation key={user.email} email={user.email} status={status ?? { stage: 'unclaimed' }} onComplete={reload} />
    : surveyAssignmentId ? <SurveyLinkPage key={`${user.email}-${surveyAssignmentId}`} assignmentId={surveyAssignmentId}/>
    : <div className="workspace"><StudentDashboard /></div>}
    </Suspense>
  </PortalShell>;
}
components/student-surveys.tsx

修改前（完整檔案）

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { appUrl } from '@/lib/routes';
import { availabilityLabel, formatSurveyTime, surveyLink, validateSurveyAnswers, type SurveyAssignment, type SurveySummary } from '@/lib/surveys';
import '@/app/surveys.css';

export function StudentSurveys({ studentId }: { studentId: string }) {
  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<SurveySummary[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setError('');
    rpc<{ assignments: SurveySummary[] }>('nptc_survey_student_data', { requested_student: studentId }, abort.signal)
      .then(data => setAssignments(data.assignments)).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [studentId, reload]);
  return <section className="survey-section" aria-label="問卷填寫公告">
    <div className="survey-heading"><span className="section-label">QUESTIONNAIRE STATUS</span><h2>問卷填寫公告</h2><p>這裡只公告問卷是否完成填寫。</p></div>
    {error && <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新讀取</Button></p>}
    {!error && assignments === null && <p role="status">正在讀取問卷完成狀態…</p>}
    {!error && assignments?.length === 0 && <p className="survey-muted">本梯次目前沒有問卷公告。</p>}
    <ul className="survey-completion-list">{assignments?.map(a => <li className="survey-completion-row" key={a.id}><div><h3>{a.title}</h3><small className="survey-muted">發布日期：{formatSurveyTime(a.created_at)}</small>{a.submitted_at && <small className="survey-muted">完成時間：{formatSurveyTime(a.submitted_at)}</small>}</div><div className="survey-completion-actions"><span className={`survey-badge ${a.submitted_at ? 'done' : ''}`}>{a.submitted_at ? '已完成填寫' : '尚未完成填寫'}</span>{!a.submitted_at && (a.availability === 'open' ? <a className="text-link" href={appUrl('/student/') + surveyLink(a.id)}>前往問卷 ↗</a> : <span className="survey-muted">{availabilityLabel[a.availability]}</span>)}</div></li>)}</ul>
  </section>;
}

export function SurveyLinkPage({ assignmentId }: { assignmentId: string }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<{ assignment: SurveyAssignment; workshopName: string } | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(''), [receipt, setReceipt] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setError('');
    rpc<{ assignment: SurveyAssignment; workshopName: string }>('nptc_survey_form', { requested_assignment: assignmentId }, abort.signal)
      .then(setData).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [assignmentId, reload]);
  return <div className="workspace survey-link-page"><div className="survey-heading"><span className="section-label">QUESTIONNAIRE</span><h1>問卷填寫</h1>{data && <p>{data.workshopName}</p>}<a className="text-link" href={appUrl('/student/')}>返回我的 OSCE 成績</a></div>
    {error ? <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新讀取</Button></p> : !data ? <p role="status">正在讀取問卷…</p> : receipt || data.assignment.submitted_at ? <section className="survey-card survey-receipt" role="status"><h2>{data.assignment.definition.title}</h2><strong>已完成填寫</strong><p>感謝您的作答，問卷已繳交。</p><p className="survey-muted">提交時間：{formatSurveyTime(receipt || data.assignment.submitted_at)}</p><a className="text-link" href={appUrl('/student/')}>返回查看問卷完成公告</a></section> : data.assignment.availability !== 'open' ? <section className="survey-card"><h2>{data.assignment.definition.title}</h2><p>{availabilityLabel[data.assignment.availability ?? 'closed']}</p>{data.assignment.opens_at && <p className="survey-muted">開放時間：{formatSurveyTime(data.assignment.opens_at)}</p>}{data.assignment.closes_at && <p className="survey-muted">截止時間：{formatSurveyTime(data.assignment.closes_at)}</p>}</section> : <SurveyForm key={data.assignment.id} assignment={data.assignment} onComplete={setReceipt} />}
  </div>;
}

function SurveyForm({ assignment, onComplete }: { assignment: SurveyAssignment; onComplete: (time: string) => void }) {
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<(number | null)[]>(() => assignment.definition.items.map(() => null));
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState(''), [uncertain, setUncertain] = useState(false);
  const pending = useRef<{ id: string; answers: string } | null>(null);
  const min = assignment.definition.minScore ?? 1, max = assignment.definition.maxScore ?? 6;
  const options = Array.from({ length: max - min + 1 }, (_, i) => min + i);
  async function submit() {
    setError('');
    try {
      validateSurveyAnswers(answers, assignment.definition.items.length, min, max);
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(answers);
      if (!pending.current) pending.current = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), answers: serialized };
      if (pending.current.answers !== serialized) throw new Error('正在確認上一筆提交，請重試原作答。');
      setBusy(true);
      const result = await rpc<{ ok: true; submitted_at: string }>('nptc_survey_submit', { body: { assignmentId: assignment.id, version: assignment.scale_version, requestId: pending.current.id, answers } }, AbortSignal.timeout(15000));
      onComplete(result.submitted_at);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (pending.current) setUncertain(true);
      setError((cause as Error).name === 'AbortError' ? '連線逾時，尚無法確認是否已提交。請重試原作答或重新開啟此連結確認。' : (cause as Error).message);
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <form className="survey-form" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); void submit(); }}>
    <header><h2>{assignment.definition.title}</h2>{assignment.definition.notice && <p className="survey-warning">{assignment.definition.notice}</p>}</header>
    <div className="survey-instructions">{assignment.definition.instructions.map(p => <p key={p}>{p}</p>)}<p>每題選擇一個答案。繳交後保留原始作答，不提供修改。</p></div>
    <fieldset disabled={busy || uncertain} className="survey-items">{assignment.definition.items.map((item, i) => <fieldset className="survey-question" key={i}><legend>第 {i + 1} 題</legend>
      {item.text ? <p className="survey-statement-text">{item.text}</p> : <div className="survey-statements"><p><span>左側敘述（{min}）</span>{item.left}</p><p><span>右側敘述（{max}）</span>{item.right}</p></div>}
      <div className="survey-options" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${Math.min(options.length, 6)}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }} role="radiogroup" aria-label={`第 ${i + 1} 題，${min} 至 ${max} 單選`}>{options.map(value => <label key={value}><input type="radio" name={`survey-q${i + 1}`} value={value} required checked={answers[i] === value} onChange={() => setAnswers(current => current.map((n, j) => i === j ? value : n))} /><span>{value}</span></label>)}</div>
      <div className="survey-scale-labels"><span>{assignment.definition.leftLabel ?? (item.left ? '最接近左側' : '')}</span><span>{assignment.definition.rightLabel ?? (item.right ? '最接近右側' : '')}</span></div>
    </fieldset>)}</fieldset>
    <div className="survey-submit-bar"><span aria-live="polite">已填 {answers.filter(n => n !== null).length} / {answers.length} 題</span><Button className="action" disabled={busy} type="submit">{busy ? '繳交中…' : uncertain ? '重試原作答' : '繳交問卷'}</Button></div>
    {uncertain && <p className="survey-muted">為避免重複紀錄，已暫停編輯。重試會使用相同作答與提交編號。</p>}{error && <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
  </form>;
}

修改後（完整檔案）

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { appUrl } from '@/lib/routes';
import { availabilityLabel, formatSurveyTime, surveyLink, validateSurveyResponse, type SurveyAnswer, type SurveyAssignment, type SurveySummary } from '@/lib/surveys';
import '@/app/surveys.css';

export function StudentSurveys({ studentId }: { studentId: string }) {
  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<SurveySummary[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setError('');
    rpc<{ assignments: SurveySummary[] }>('nptc_survey_student_data', { requested_student: studentId }, abort.signal)
      .then(data => setAssignments(data.assignments)).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [studentId, reload]);
  return <section className="survey-section" aria-label="問卷填寫公告">
    <div className="survey-heading"><span className="section-label">QUESTIONNAIRE STATUS</span><h2>問卷填寫公告</h2><p>這裡只公告問卷是否完成填寫。</p></div>
    {error && <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新讀取</Button></p>}
    {!error && assignments === null && <p role="status">正在讀取問卷完成狀態…</p>}
    {!error && assignments?.length === 0 && <p className="survey-muted">本梯次目前沒有問卷公告。</p>}
    <ul className="survey-completion-list">{assignments?.map(a => <li className="survey-completion-row" key={a.id}><div><h3>{a.title}</h3><small className="survey-muted">發布日期：{formatSurveyTime(a.created_at)}</small>{a.submitted_at && <small className="survey-muted">完成時間：{formatSurveyTime(a.submitted_at)}</small>}</div><div className="survey-completion-actions"><span className={`survey-badge ${a.submitted_at ? 'done' : ''}`}>{a.submitted_at ? '已完成填寫' : '尚未完成填寫'}</span>{!a.submitted_at && (a.availability === 'open' ? <a className="text-link" href={appUrl('/student/') + surveyLink(a.id)}>前往問卷 ↗</a> : <span className="survey-muted">{availabilityLabel[a.availability]}</span>)}</div></li>)}</ul>
  </section>;
}

export function SurveyLinkPage({ assignmentId }: { assignmentId: string }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<{ assignment: SurveyAssignment; workshopName: string } | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(''), [receipt, setReceipt] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setError('');
    rpc<{ assignment: SurveyAssignment; workshopName: string }>('nptc_survey_form', { requested_assignment: assignmentId }, abort.signal)
      .then(setData).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [assignmentId, reload]);
  return <div className="workspace survey-link-page"><div className="survey-heading"><span className="section-label">QUESTIONNAIRE</span><h1>問卷填寫</h1>{data && <p>{data.workshopName}</p>}<a className="text-link" href={appUrl('/student/')}>返回我的 OSCE 成績</a></div>
    {error ? <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新讀取</Button></p> : !data ? <p role="status">正在讀取問卷…</p> : receipt || data.assignment.submitted_at ? <section className="survey-card survey-receipt" role="status"><h2>{data.assignment.definition.title}</h2><strong>已完成填寫</strong><p>感謝您的作答，問卷已繳交。</p><p className="survey-muted">提交時間：{formatSurveyTime(receipt || data.assignment.submitted_at)}</p><a className="text-link" href={appUrl('/student/')}>返回查看問卷完成公告</a></section> : data.assignment.availability !== 'open' ? <section className="survey-card"><h2>{data.assignment.definition.title}</h2><p>{availabilityLabel[data.assignment.availability ?? 'closed']}</p>{data.assignment.opens_at && <p className="survey-muted">開放時間：{formatSurveyTime(data.assignment.opens_at)}</p>}{data.assignment.closes_at && <p className="survey-muted">截止時間：{formatSurveyTime(data.assignment.closes_at)}</p>}</section> : <SurveyForm key={data.assignment.id} assignment={data.assignment} onComplete={setReceipt} />}
  </div>;
}

function SurveyForm({ assignment, onComplete }: { assignment: SurveyAssignment; onComplete: (time: string) => void }) {
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<SurveyAnswer[]>(() => assignment.definition.items.map(() => null));
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState(''), [uncertain, setUncertain] = useState(false);
  const pending = useRef<{ id: string; answers: string } | null>(null);
  const min = assignment.definition.minScore ?? 1, max = assignment.definition.maxScore ?? 6;
  const options = Array.from({ length: max - min + 1 }, (_, i) => min + i);
  async function submit() {
    setError('');
    try {
      validateSurveyResponse(answers, assignment.definition);
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(answers);
      if (!pending.current) pending.current = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), answers: serialized };
      if (pending.current.answers !== serialized) throw new Error('正在確認上一筆提交，請重試原作答。');
      setBusy(true);
      const result = await rpc<{ ok: true; submitted_at: string }>('nptc_survey_submit', { body: { assignmentId: assignment.id, version: assignment.scale_version, requestId: pending.current.id, answers } }, AbortSignal.timeout(15000));
      onComplete(result.submitted_at);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (pending.current) setUncertain(true);
      setError((cause as Error).name === 'AbortError' ? '連線逾時，尚無法確認是否已提交。請重試原作答或重新開啟此連結確認。' : (cause as Error).message);
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <form className="survey-form" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); void submit(); }}>
    <header><h2>{assignment.definition.title}</h2>{assignment.definition.notice && <p className="survey-warning">{assignment.definition.notice}</p>}</header>
    <div className="survey-instructions">{assignment.definition.instructions.map(p => <p key={p}>{p}</p>)}<p>每題選擇一個答案。繳交後保留原始作答，不提供修改。</p></div>
    <fieldset disabled={busy || uncertain} className="survey-items">{assignment.definition.items.map((item, i) => <fieldset className="survey-question" key={i}><legend>第 {i + 1} 題{item.required === false ? '（選填）' : ''}</legend>
      {item.section && <h3 className="survey-domain-heading">{item.section}</h3>}
      {item.text ? <p className="survey-statement-text">{item.text}</p> : <div className="survey-statements"><p><span>左側敘述（{min}）</span>{item.left}</p><p><span>右側敘述（{max}）</span>{item.right}</p></div>}
      {item.type === 'text' ? <textarea aria-label={`第 ${i + 1} 題文字意見`} rows={4} maxLength={item.maxLength ?? 2000} required={item.required !== false} value={String(answers[i] ?? '')} onChange={event => setAnswers(current => current.map((n, j) => i === j ? event.target.value : n))}/> : item.type === 'choice' ? <div className="survey-choice-options" role="radiogroup" aria-label={`第 ${i + 1} 題單選`}>{item.options?.map(value => <label key={value}><input type="radio" name={`survey-q${i + 1}`} value={value} required={item.required !== false} checked={answers[i] === value} onChange={() => setAnswers(current => current.map((n, j) => i === j ? value : n))}/><span>{value}</span></label>)}</div> : <><div className={`survey-options${assignment.definition.optionLabels ? ' survey-options-labeled' : ''}`} style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${Math.min(options.length, 6)}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }} role="radiogroup" aria-label={`第 ${i + 1} 題，${min} 至 ${max} 單選`}>{options.map(value => <label key={value}><input type="radio" name={`survey-q${i + 1}`} value={value} required={item.required !== false} checked={answers[i] === value} onChange={() => setAnswers(current => current.map((n, j) => i === j ? value : n))} /><span>{value}</span>{assignment.definition.optionLabels?.[value] && <small>{assignment.definition.optionLabels[value]}</small>}</label>)}</div>
      <div className="survey-scale-labels"><span>{assignment.definition.leftLabel ?? (item.left ? '最接近左側' : '')}</span><span>{assignment.definition.rightLabel ?? (item.right ? '最接近右側' : '')}</span></div></>}
    </fieldset>)}</fieldset>
    <div className="survey-submit-bar"><span aria-live="polite">已填 {answers.filter(n => n !== null && n !== '').length} / {answers.length} 題{assignment.definition.items.some(q => q.required === false) && `（${assignment.definition.items.filter(q => q.required === false).length} 題選填）`}</span><Button className="action" disabled={busy} type="submit">{busy ? '繳交中…' : uncertain ? '重試原作答' : '繳交問卷'}</Button></div>
    {uncertain && <p className="survey-muted">為避免重複紀錄，已暫停編輯。重試會使用相同作答與提交編號。</p>}{error && <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
  </form>;
}
components/survey-analysis.tsx

修改前（完整檔案）

import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { formatSurveyTime } from '@/lib/surveys';
import { dimensionLabel, metricRange, metricValue, orderedAssignments, scaleKey, surveyPeople, surveyTrend, type SurveyAnalysisData, type SurveyTrendPoint } from '@/lib/survey-analysis';
import '@/app/learning-analysis.css';

const scoreText = (value: number | null) => value === null ? '—' : Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
const dateText = (value: string) => value && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)) ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat('zh-TW', { timeZone: 'Asia/Taipei', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date(value)) : '未設定';

export default function SurveyAnalysis({ currentWorkshopId }: { currentWorkshopId?: string }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<SurveyAnalysisData | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  const [workshopId, setWorkshopId] = useState(currentWorkshopId ?? 'all'), [selectedScale, setSelectedScale] = useState('');
  const [personId, setPersonId] = useState('all'), [query, setQuery] = useState(''), [metric, setMetric] = useState('total');
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<{ assignmentId: string; studentId: string } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setData(null); setError('');
    rpc<SurveyAnalysisData>('nptc_survey_analysis', { requested_workshop: null }, abort.signal)
      .then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setData(result); })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [reload]);
  const effectiveWorkshop = data?.workshops.some(w => w.id === workshopId) ? workshopId : 'all';
  const scopeAssignments = useMemo(() => data?.assignments.filter(a => a.status !== 'draft' && (effectiveWorkshop === 'all' || a.workshop_id === effectiveWorkshop)) ?? [], [data, effectiveWorkshop]);
  const scales = [...new Map(scopeAssignments.map(a => [scaleKey(a), { key: scaleKey(a), title: a.definition.title, version: a.scale_version, definition: a.definition }])).values()];
  const scale = scales.find(s => s.key === selectedScale) ?? scales[0];
  const assignments = orderedAssignments(scopeAssignments.filter(a => scale && scaleKey(a) === scale.key));
  const people = data ? surveyPeople(data, assignments) : [];
  const searchedPeople = people.filter(p => p.names.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()));
  const person = searchedPeople.find(p => p.identity === personId);
  const displayedPeople = person ? [person] : searchedPeople;
  const metricOptions = [{ key: 'total', label: '總分' }, ...Object.keys(scale?.definition.dimensions ?? {}).map(name => ({ key: `dimension:${name}`, label: dimensionLabel(name) }))];
  const activeMetric = metricOptions.some(m => m.key === metric) ? metric : 'total';
  const metricLabel = metricOptions.find(m => m.key === activeMetric)?.label ?? '總分';
  const range = scale ? metricRange(scale.definition, activeMetric) : { min: 0, max: 1 };
  const points = data ? surveyTrend(data, assignments, displayedPeople, activeMetric) : [];
  const identities = new Set(displayedPeople.map(p => p.identity)), assignmentIds = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.id));
  const responses = data?.responses.filter(r => assignmentIds.has(r.assignment_id) && identities.has(r.identity)) ?? [];
  const values = responses.map(r => metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric)).filter((n): n is number => n !== null);
  const expected = assignments.reduce((sum, a) => sum + displayedPeople.filter(p => p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)).length, 0);
  const detailResponse = detail ? responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === detail.assignmentId && r.student_id === detail.studentId) : undefined;
  const detailAssignment = detailResponse ? assignments.find(a => a.id === detailResponse.assignment_id) : undefined;
  const detailPerson = detailResponse ? displayedPeople.find(p => p.identity === detailResponse.identity) : undefined;
  const resetDetails = () => { setPersonId('all'); setDetail(null); };

  if (!data) return <section className="analysis-state" role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'}><BarChart3 size={32}/><h1>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>{error || '正在讀取學員量表紀錄…'}</p>{error && <button className="action" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新載入</button>}</section>;
  return <div className="learning-analysis survey-learning-analysis">
    <header className="analysis-heading"><div><span className="section-label">SURVEY ANALYTICS</span><h1><BarChart3 aria-hidden="true"/>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>查詢學員的問卷得分，查看同一份量表的歷次填寫變化。</p></div><button className="action secondary" onClick={() => { setDetail(null); setReload(v => v + 1); }}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true"/>更新量表資料</button></header>
    <section className="analysis-controls" aria-label="量表分析篩選"><div className="survey-analysis-filters">
      <label>OSCE 梯次<select value={effectiveWorkshop} onChange={e => { setWorkshopId(e.target.value); setSelectedScale(''); resetDetails(); }}><option value="all">所有可檢視梯次</option>{data.workshops.map(w => <option value={w.id} key={w.id}>{w.name}{w.archived_at ? '（已封存）' : ''}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>選擇量表<select value={scale?.key ?? ''} disabled={!scales.length} onChange={e => { setSelectedScale(e.target.value); setMetric('total'); resetDetails(); }}>{!scales.length && <option value="">尚無量表發布</option>}{scales.map(s => <option key={s.key} value={s.key}>{s.title} · {s.version}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>查詢學員<input type="search" value={query} placeholder="輸入學員姓名" onChange={e => { setQuery(e.target.value); resetDetails(); }}/></label>
      <label>學員<select value={person?.identity ?? 'all'} onChange={e => { setPersonId(e.target.value); setDetail(null); }}><option value="all">全部符合條件的學員</option>{searchedPeople.map(p => <option key={p.identity} value={p.identity}>{p.names}（{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次）</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>分數項目<select value={activeMetric} onChange={e => setMetric(e.target.value)}>{metricOptions.map(m => <option key={m.key} value={m.key}>{m.label}</option>)}</select></label>
    </div><p className="form-help">{data.includesArchived ? '目前可包含已封存梯次。' : '目前僅包含未封存梯次。'}不同量表版本分開呈現；未完成問卷不計為 0 分。</p></section>
    {!assignments.length ? <section className="analysis-panel analysis-empty"><h2>目前尚無可分析的問卷</h2><p>在量表管理庫加入問卷並收回學員作答後，即可查看分數與趨勢。</p></section> : <>
      {scale?.definition.notice && <p className="analysis-note">{scale.definition.notice}</p>}
      <section className="analysis-metrics" aria-label="量表填答摘要"><article><span>學員人數</span><strong>{displayedPeople.length}</strong><small>符合目前篩選條件</small></article><article><span>完成問卷／應填份數</span><strong>{responses.length} / {expected}</strong><small>各次發布分開計算</small></article><article><span>{metricLabel}平均</span><strong>{scoreText(values.length ? values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length : null)}</strong><small>依 {values.length} 筆有效分數計算</small></article><article><span>同版量表發布次數</span><strong>{assignments.length}</strong><small>{metricLabel}量尺範圍 {range.min}–{range.max} 分</small></article></section>
      <section className="analysis-panel"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{person ? `${person.names}｜歷次${metricLabel}趨勢` : `各次發布｜${metricLabel}平均趨勢`}</h2><p>依發布時間排列，橫軸使用教師備註；未填備註時以發布次序顯示。</p></div>{person && <button className="analysis-link" onClick={resetDetails}>查看全部學員</button>}</div>
        <SurveyTrendChart points={points} range={range} metricLabel={metricLabel} personal={!!person}/>
        <div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-trend-summary"><thead><tr><th>發布備註</th><th>OSCE 梯次／發布時間</th><th>{person ? metricLabel : `${metricLabel}平均`}</th><th>有效分數筆數</th></tr></thead><tbody>{points.map(p => <tr key={p.id}><th scope="row">{p.label}</th><td>{p.workshop}<small>{formatSurveyTime(p.date)}</small></td><td>{scoreText(p.value)}</td><td>{p.count}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
        <p className="analysis-note">{person ? '折線呈現此學員在各次發布的填答分數；缺少作答的資料點留空。' : '每個資料點是該次發布中符合篩選學員的平均分數，人數可能不同；可選擇單一學員查看個人趨勢。'}分數變化本身不代表教學效果或能力合格判定。</p>
      </section>
      <section className="analysis-panel" aria-label="學員量表分數"><h2>學員量表分數</h2><p>點選姓名可查看個人趨勢；點選分數可查看該次問卷的逐題原答與換算分數。</p><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-student-scores"><thead><tr><th>學員</th>{points.map(p => <th key={p.id}>{p.label}<small>{p.workshop} · {dateText(p.date)}</small></th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>{displayedPeople.map(p => <tr key={p.identity}><th scope="row"><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail(null); }}>{p.names}</button><small>{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次紀錄</small></th>{assignments.map(a => {
        if (!p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)) return <td key={a.id}><span className="survey-muted">不適用</span></td>;
        const r = data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.identity === p.identity), value = r ? metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric) : null;
        return <td key={a.id}>{r ? <><button className="survey-score-button" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail({ assignmentId: a.id, studentId: r.student_id }); }}>{value === null ? '已完成' : `${scoreText(value)} 分`}</button><small>填寫：{formatSurveyTime(r.submitted_at)}</small></> : <span className="survey-muted">尚未填寫</span>}</td>;
      })}</tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!displayedPeople.length && <p className="analysis-empty">目前沒有符合條件的學員。</p>}</section>
      {detailResponse && detailAssignment && <section className="analysis-panel survey-answer-detail" aria-label="逐題量表作答"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{detailPerson?.names}｜逐題作答</h2><p>{detailAssignment.definition.title} · {detailAssignment.note || '未填備註'} · {formatSurveyTime(detailResponse.submitted_at)}</p></div><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => setDetail(null)}>關閉明細</button></div><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>題號</th><th>題目內容</th><th>原始作答</th><th>換算得分</th></tr></thead><tbody>{detailAssignment.definition.items.map((q, i) => <tr key={i}><th scope="row">{i + 1}</th><td className="survey-answer-statement">{q.text || <><p>左：{q.left}</p><p>右：{q.right}</p></>}</td><td>{detailResponse.answers[i] ?? '—'}</td><td>{detailResponse.scores.itemScores[i] ?? '—'}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div><p className="analysis-note">總分：{scoreText(detailResponse.scores.total)}。資料以該次問卷保存的題目與計分版本呈現。</p></section>}
    </>}
  </div>;
}

function SurveyTrendChart({ points, range, metricLabel, personal }: { points: SurveyTrendPoint[]; range: { min: number; max: number }; metricLabel: string; personal: boolean }) {
  const id = useId();
  const filled = points.filter(p => p.value !== null);
  if (!filled.length) return <p className="analysis-empty" role="status">目前沒有可繪製的{metricLabel}紀錄，尚未填答不會畫成 0 分。</p>;
  const width = 840, height = 316, left = 64, right = 30, top = 32, bottom = 72;
  const low = Math.min(range.min, ...filled.map(p => p.value!)), high = Math.max(range.max, ...filled.map(p => p.value!));
  const span = high - low || 1;
  const x = (i: number) => left + (points.length === 1 ? (width - left - right) / 2 : i * (width - left - right) / (points.length - 1));
  const y = (value: number) => top + (high - value) * (height - top - bottom) / span;
  const segments: { x: number; y: number }[][] = [];
  let segment: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
  points.forEach((p, i) => { if (p.value === null) { if (segment.length) segments.push(segment); segment = []; } else segment.push({ x: x(i), y: y(p.value) }); });
  if (segment.length) segments.push(segment);
  const step = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(points.length / 8));
  return <figure className="survey-trend-figure"><div className="survey-trend-chart-scroll"><svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} role="img" aria-labelledby={`${id}-title ${id}-desc`}><title id={`${id}-title`}>{personal ? '學員' : '各次發布平均'}{metricLabel}趨勢</title><desc id={`${id}-desc`}>{filled.length} 個有效資料點。完整數值列於下方表格，缺漏紀錄不連線。</desc>
    {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map(i => { const value = low + span * i / 4; return <g key={i}><line x1={left} x2={width - right} y1={y(value)} y2={y(value)} stroke="#dce7dc" strokeDasharray={i === 0 ? undefined : '4 5'}/><text x={left - 13} y={y(value) + 4} textAnchor="end" fill="#5c7368" fontSize={12}>{scoreText(value)}</text></g>; })}
    <text x={left} y={16} fill="#365d4c" fontSize={12}>{metricLabel}（分）</text>
    {segments.filter(s => s.length > 1).map((s, i) => <polyline key={i} fill="none" stroke="#2c7962" strokeWidth={3} strokeLinejoin="round" points={s.map(p => `${p.x},${p.y}`).join(' ')}/>)}
    {points.map((p, i) => <g key={p.id}>{p.value !== null && <g className="survey-trend-point" tabIndex={0} role="img" aria-label={`${p.label}，${metricLabel} ${scoreText(p.value)}，${p.count} 筆`}><title>{p.label}｜{p.workshop}｜{scoreText(p.value)} 分｜n={p.count}</title><circle cx={x(i)} cy={y(p.value)} r={6} fill="#fff" stroke="#2c7962" strokeWidth={3}/>{points.length <= 14 && <text x={x(i)} y={y(p.value) - 13} textAnchor="middle" fill="#164a38" fontSize={12} fontWeight={600}>{scoreText(p.value)}</text>}</g>}{(i % step === 0 || i === points.length - 1) && <><text x={x(i)} y={height - bottom + 24} textAnchor={points.length === 1 ? 'middle' : i === 0 ? 'start' : i === points.length - 1 ? 'end' : 'middle'} fill="#345a49" fontSize={11}>{p.label.length > 10 ? `${p.label.slice(0, 9)}…` : p.label}</text><text x={x(i)} y={height - bottom + 42} textAnchor={points.length === 1 ? 'middle' : i === 0 ? 'start' : i === points.length - 1 ? 'end' : 'middle'} fill="#728478" fontSize={11}>{dateText(p.date)}</text></>}</g>)}
  </svg></div><figcaption>{filled.length === 1 ? '目前僅有一筆有效紀錄，暫以單一資料點呈現。' : '依同份同版量表的發布順序呈現。'}圖表下方可查看各次分數與人數。</figcaption></figure>;
}

修改後（完整檔案）

import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { formatSurveyTime } from '@/lib/surveys';
import { dimensionLabel, metricRange, metricValue, orderedAssignments, scaleKey, surveyPeople, surveyTrend, type SurveyAnalysisData, type SurveyTrendPoint } from '@/lib/survey-analysis';
import '@/app/learning-analysis.css';

const scoreText = (value: number | null) => value === null ? '—' : Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
const dateText = (value: string) => value && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)) ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat('zh-TW', { timeZone: 'Asia/Taipei', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date(value)) : '未設定';

export default function SurveyAnalysis({ currentWorkshopId }: { currentWorkshopId?: string }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<SurveyAnalysisData | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  const [workshopId, setWorkshopId] = useState(currentWorkshopId ?? 'all'), [selectedScale, setSelectedScale] = useState('');
  const [personId, setPersonId] = useState('all'), [query, setQuery] = useState(''), [metric, setMetric] = useState('total');
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<{ assignmentId: string; studentId: string } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setData(null); setError('');
    rpc<SurveyAnalysisData>('nptc_survey_analysis', { requested_workshop: null }, abort.signal)
      .then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setData(result); })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [reload]);
  const effectiveWorkshop = data?.workshops.some(w => w.id === workshopId) ? workshopId : 'all';
  const scopeAssignments = useMemo(() => data?.assignments.filter(a => a.status !== 'draft' && (effectiveWorkshop === 'all' || a.workshop_id === effectiveWorkshop)) ?? [], [data, effectiveWorkshop]);
  const scales = [...new Map(scopeAssignments.map(a => [scaleKey(a), { key: scaleKey(a), title: a.definition.title, version: a.scale_version, definition: a.definition }])).values()];
  const scale = scales.find(s => s.key === selectedScale) ?? scales[0];
  const noScores = ['pending', 'unscored'].includes(scale?.definition.scoringStatus ?? '');
  const scoringLabel = scale?.definition.scoringStatus === 'pending' ? '計分規則待確認' : '不計分（原始回覆）';
  const assignments = orderedAssignments(scopeAssignments.filter(a => scale && scaleKey(a) === scale.key));
  const people = data ? surveyPeople(data, assignments) : [];
  const searchedPeople = people.filter(p => p.names.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()));
  const person = searchedPeople.find(p => p.identity === personId);
  const displayedPeople = person ? [person] : searchedPeople;
  const metricOptions = noScores ? [{ key: 'unscored', label: scoringLabel }] : [{ key: 'total', label: '總分' }, ...Object.keys(scale?.definition.dimensions ?? {}).map(name => ({ key: `dimension:${name}`, label: dimensionLabel(name) }))];
  const activeMetric = metricOptions.some(m => m.key === metric) ? metric : metricOptions[0].key;
  const metricLabel = metricOptions.find(m => m.key === activeMetric)?.label ?? '總分';
  const range = scale ? metricRange(scale.definition, activeMetric) : { min: 0, max: 1 };
  const points = data ? surveyTrend(data, assignments, displayedPeople, activeMetric) : [];
  const identities = new Set(displayedPeople.map(p => p.identity)), assignmentIds = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.id));
  const responses = data?.responses.filter(r => assignmentIds.has(r.assignment_id) && identities.has(r.identity)) ?? [];
  const values = responses.map(r => metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric)).filter((n): n is number => n !== null);
  const expected = assignments.reduce((sum, a) => sum + displayedPeople.filter(p => p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)).length, 0);
  const detailResponse = detail ? responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === detail.assignmentId && r.student_id === detail.studentId) : undefined;
  const detailAssignment = detailResponse ? assignments.find(a => a.id === detailResponse.assignment_id) : undefined;
  const detailPerson = detailResponse ? displayedPeople.find(p => p.identity === detailResponse.identity) : undefined;
  const resetDetails = () => { setPersonId('all'); setDetail(null); };

  if (!data) return <section className="analysis-state" role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'}><BarChart3 size={32}/><h1>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>{error || '正在讀取學員量表紀錄…'}</p>{error && <button className="action" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新載入</button>}</section>;
  return <div className="learning-analysis survey-learning-analysis">
    <header className="analysis-heading"><div><span className="section-label">SURVEY ANALYTICS</span><h1><BarChart3 aria-hidden="true"/>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>查詢學員的問卷得分，查看同一份量表的歷次填寫變化。</p></div><button className="action secondary" onClick={() => { setDetail(null); setReload(v => v + 1); }}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true"/>更新量表資料</button></header>
    <section className="analysis-controls" aria-label="量表分析篩選"><div className="survey-analysis-filters">
      <label>OSCE 梯次<select value={effectiveWorkshop} onChange={e => { setWorkshopId(e.target.value); setSelectedScale(''); resetDetails(); }}><option value="all">所有可檢視梯次</option>{data.workshops.map(w => <option value={w.id} key={w.id}>{w.name}{w.archived_at ? '（已封存）' : ''}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>選擇量表<select value={scale?.key ?? ''} disabled={!scales.length} onChange={e => { setSelectedScale(e.target.value); setMetric('total'); resetDetails(); }}>{!scales.length && <option value="">尚無量表發布</option>}{scales.map(s => <option key={s.key} value={s.key}>{s.title} · {s.version}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>查詢學員<input type="search" value={query} placeholder="輸入學員姓名" onChange={e => { setQuery(e.target.value); resetDetails(); }}/></label>
      <label>學員<select value={person?.identity ?? 'all'} onChange={e => { setPersonId(e.target.value); setDetail(null); }}><option value="all">全部符合條件的學員</option>{searchedPeople.map(p => <option key={p.identity} value={p.identity}>{p.names}（{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次）</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>分數項目<select value={activeMetric} disabled={noScores} onChange={e => setMetric(e.target.value)}>{metricOptions.map(m => <option key={m.key} value={m.key}>{m.label}</option>)}</select></label>
    </div><p className="form-help">{data.includesArchived ? '目前可包含已封存梯次。' : '目前僅包含未封存梯次。'}不同量表版本分開呈現；未完成問卷不計為 0 分。</p></section>
    {!assignments.length ? <section className="analysis-panel analysis-empty"><h2>目前尚無可分析的問卷</h2><p>在量表管理庫加入問卷並收回學員作答後，即可查看分數與趨勢。</p></section> : <>
      {scale?.definition.notice && <p className="analysis-note">{scale.definition.notice}</p>}
      <section className="analysis-metrics" aria-label="量表填答摘要"><article><span>學員人數</span><strong>{displayedPeople.length}</strong><small>符合目前篩選條件</small></article><article><span>完成問卷／應填份數</span><strong>{responses.length} / {expected}</strong><small>各次發布分開計算</small></article><article><span>{noScores ? '計分狀態' : `${metricLabel}平均`}</span><strong>{noScores ? '—' : scoreText(values.length ? values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length : null)}</strong><small>{noScores ? scoringLabel : `依 ${values.length} 筆有效分數計算`}</small></article><article><span>同版量表發布次數</span><strong>{assignments.length}</strong><small>{noScores ? '保存原始作答與完成紀錄' : `${metricLabel}量尺範圍 ${range.min}–${range.max} 分`}</small></article></section>
      {noScores ? <section className="analysis-panel" role="status"><h2>{scoringLabel}</h2><p>目前提供完成狀態與逐題原始作答，不產生換算分數、構面分數、總分或分數趨勢。</p></section> : <section className="analysis-panel"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{person ? `${person.names}｜歷次${metricLabel}趨勢` : `各次發布｜${metricLabel}平均趨勢`}</h2><p>依發布時間排列，橫軸使用教師備註；未填備註時以發布次序顯示。</p></div>{person && <button className="analysis-link" onClick={resetDetails}>查看全部學員</button>}</div>
        <SurveyTrendChart points={points} range={range} metricLabel={metricLabel} personal={!!person}/>
        <div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-trend-summary"><thead><tr><th>發布備註</th><th>OSCE 梯次／發布時間</th><th>{person ? metricLabel : `${metricLabel}平均`}</th><th>有效分數筆數</th></tr></thead><tbody>{points.map(p => <tr key={p.id}><th scope="row">{p.label}</th><td>{p.workshop}<small>{formatSurveyTime(p.date)}</small></td><td>{scoreText(p.value)}</td><td>{p.count}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
        <p className="analysis-note">{person ? '折線呈現此學員在各次發布的填答分數；缺少作答的資料點留空。' : '每個資料點是該次發布中符合篩選學員的平均分數，人數可能不同；可選擇單一學員查看個人趨勢。'}分數變化本身不代表教學效果或能力合格判定。</p>
      </section>}
      <section className="analysis-panel" aria-label={noScores ? '學員原始作答' : '學員量表分數'}><h2>{noScores ? '學員原始作答' : '學員量表分數'}</h2><p>{noScores ? '點選「查看原答」可閱讀該次問卷的完整原始作答。' : '點選姓名可查看個人趨勢；點選分數可查看該次問卷的逐題原答與換算分數。'}</p><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-student-scores"><thead><tr><th>學員</th>{points.map(p => <th key={p.id}>{p.label}<small>{p.workshop} · {dateText(p.date)}</small></th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>{displayedPeople.map(p => <tr key={p.identity}><th scope="row"><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail(null); }}>{p.names}</button><small>{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次紀錄</small></th>{assignments.map(a => {
        if (!p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)) return <td key={a.id}><span className="survey-muted">不適用</span></td>;
        const r = data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.identity === p.identity), value = r ? metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric) : null;
        return <td key={a.id}>{r ? <><button className="survey-score-button" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail({ assignmentId: a.id, studentId: r.student_id }); }}>{noScores ? '查看原答' : value === null ? '已完成' : `${scoreText(value)} 分`}</button><small>填寫：{formatSurveyTime(r.submitted_at)}</small></> : <span className="survey-muted">尚未填寫</span>}</td>;
      })}</tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!displayedPeople.length && <p className="analysis-empty">目前沒有符合條件的學員。</p>}</section>
      {detailResponse && detailAssignment && <section className="analysis-panel survey-answer-detail" aria-label="逐題量表作答"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{detailPerson?.names}｜逐題作答</h2><p>{detailAssignment.definition.title} · {detailAssignment.note || '未填備註'} · {formatSurveyTime(detailResponse.submitted_at)}</p></div><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => setDetail(null)}>關閉明細</button></div><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>題號</th><th>題目內容</th><th>原始作答</th><th>換算得分</th></tr></thead><tbody>{detailAssignment.definition.items.map((q, i) => <tr key={i}><th scope="row">{i + 1}</th><td className="survey-answer-statement">{q.text || <><p>左：{q.left}</p><p>右：{q.right}</p></>}</td><td>{detailResponse.answers[i] ?? '—'}</td><td>{noScores ? scoringLabel : detailResponse.scores.itemScores[i] ?? '—'}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div><p className="analysis-note">{noScores ? scoringLabel : `總分：${scoreText(detailResponse.scores.total)}`}。資料以該次問卷保存的題目與計分版本呈現。</p></section>}
    </>}
  </div>;
}

  const id = useId();
  const filled = points.filter(p => p.value !== null);

  if (segment.length) segments.push(segment);

  return <figure className="survey-trend-figure"><div className="survey-trend-chart-scroll"><svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} role="img" aria-labelledby={`${id}-title ${id}-desc`}><title id={`${id}-title`}>{personal ?
